# KTM 125 SX 2027: aftermarket parts

4353 listings whose stated fitment names this bike: 343 researched parts (with what they change and rider findings), 1496 dealer and retailer listings specific to this bike, and 2514 universal retailer listings (oil, grips, batteries and the like, counted below; every listing is in [parts.csv](../parts.csv)). Research candidates: check fitment on the maker's page before relying on a row. Back to [the family catalog](../CATALOG.md).
